I keep putting those things off, and I don't really know why I don't want to do them anymore.\n\nDoctor: How long have you been feeling this way about the things you used to enjoy?\n\nPatient: It's been a while now. Um, I'd say it started right after a robbery at this festival job I was working. I just barely care about it while I'm doing it, so... no, it doesn't give me that same good feeling it used to.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel sad or empty most of the time?\n\nPatient: No, not really. I wouldn't say I feel sad or empty most days. It's more just... that lack of interest I mentioned. I just don't feel much of a drive to do things, but I don't walk around feeling sad all the time.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed any significant changes in your weight since you started feeling this way?\n\nPatient: No, not really. My weight has stayed pretty much the same, honestly. I haven't noticed any big drops or gains or anything like that.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed any changes in how active or sluggish you feel compared to before you started feeling this way about activities?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't really noticed anything like that. My actual energy levels are pretty much normal.\n\nDoctor: Do you often feel guilty or worthless about things?\n\nPatient: No, I don't. I don't really feel guilty about things, and I wouldn't say I feel worthless, either. That hasn't been an issue for me.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever think about death or dying?\n\nPatient: No, I don't. I haven't had any thoughts about dying or... death, or anything like that. It just hasn't crossed my mind.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed any changes in how restless or fidgety you feel compared to before?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't noticed anything like that. Like I mentioned a minute ago, I don't really feel restless. I'm not fidgety or pacing around or anything.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Ask about exactly ONE thing. Like I was saying before, they really just feel like chores to me now. Even if I do force myself to go on a hike or show up to game night, I don't get anything out of it. I just barely care about it while I'm doing it, so... no, it doesn't give me that same good feeling it used to.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel sad or empty most of the time?\n\nPatient: No, not really.
